Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on particles structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 343. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 975 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to particles structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 343/975 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
